To this end, we propose a novel Knowledge-Driven Ordinal-Aware Learning (KOAL) framework with three synergistic modules. Specifically, the Clinical-Context Modulation (CCM) module uses clinical variables (e.g., age and PSA) to dynamically modulate discriminative image representations. The Knowledge-Guided Prototype Alignment (KGPA) module leverages an LLM to extract group-specific expert knowledge from training radiology reports and clinical guidelines, producing offline semantic anchors describing grade-specific radiological findings without requiring patient-specific reports at inference. Through prototype contrastive alignment, patient-specific mpMRI representations are matched with these anchors to promote pathology-aligned representation learning. The Hierarchical Ordinal-aware Constraints (HOC) module decouples primary and secondary Gleason pattern prediction and maps their probabilistic outputs to GGG via a Differentiable Bio-logic Mapping Layer (DBML), ensuring pathological grading consistency. Experiments on public PI-CAI and in-house datasets demonstrate that KOAL outperforms state-of-the-art methods. Code is available at: https://github.com/Gother-GZ/KOAL.

</details>


### [36] [Why does Deep Learning Improve Visual SLAM?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06023)
*Giovanni Cioffi,Davide Scaramuzza*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过控制变量实验，系统探究了深度学习视觉SLAM系统成功的关键因素，发现核心在于学习的2D数据关联与不确定性，而非循环架构本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于深度学习的视觉SLAM系统在挑战性视觉条件下性能显著优于传统几何方法，但学界对其成功的关键组件（学习的2D数据关联、不确定性、循环架构）缺乏清晰的归因分析。本文旨在厘清哪些组件从根本上贡献了性能提升。

Method: 采用控制变量实验（controlled study），分别检验三种因素：①仅学习的2D数据关联；②学习的2D数据关联+不确定性；③循环架构本身。通过消融实验对比各组件对整体性能的贡献。

Result: 实验结果表明，深度学习视觉SLAM的成功主要源于学习的2D数据关联与不确定性，而非循环架构。循环架构并非性能提升的关键驱动因素。

Conclusion: 该发现强调了学习型范式在2D数据关联和不确定性估计设计中的必要性，为未来SLAM系统架构设计提供了明确指导——应优先关注数据关联与不确定性的学习，而非单纯依赖循环网络结构。

Abstract: Visual SLAM is a well-established technology utilized in a wide range of real-world applications. However, its performance still degrades under challenging visual conditions, such as low texture, severe motion blur, and poor illumination. Systems based on deep learning outperform classical geometry-based ones and achieve state-of-the-art results by combining learned 2D data association and uncertainty with differentiable geometric optimization in recurrent architectures. Still, it remains unclear exactly which components are fundamentally responsible for this success. In this paper, we ask: Is the superior performance of deep learning-based systems driven primarily by learned 2D data association, the combination of learned 2D data association and uncertainty, or the recurrent architecture itself? We investigate this question empirically by conducting a controlled study. Our findings reveal that the success of DL-based V-SLAM systems hinges on learned 2D data association and uncertainty rather than their recurrent architecture, underscoring the necessity of learning-based paradigms for the design of these components. Upon acceptance, the code will be released as open source.

</details>


### [37] [MSA-DCNN: A Data-Efficient Multi-Scale Deformable CNN for Medical Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06083)
*Hamza Hussaini,Shahana Bano,Eyad Elyan,Carlos Francisco Moreno-García*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了MSA-DCNN，一个尺度一致的可变形注意力学习框架，通过自适应多尺度采样、尺度内显著性精炼、学习型跨尺度融合和辅助自蒸馏，在统一优化方案下解决医学图像分类中的多尺度形态和标注稀缺问题，在多个基准上取得优于ViT、CNN和半监督基线的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法在医学图像分类中处理多尺度形态和有限标注时存在不足：DCN方法缺乏显式多尺度注意力融合和标签高效正则化；多尺度架构依赖静态融合；半监督方法未联合建模自适应跨尺度表示。现有方法孤立解决这些问题，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 提出MSA-DCNN框架，包含四个核心组件：1）自适应多尺度采样（adaptive multi-scale sampling）；2）尺度内显著性精炼（within-scale saliency refinement）；3）学习型跨尺度融合（learned cross-scale fusion）；4）辅助自蒸馏（auxiliary self-distillation）。所有组件在统一优化方案中联合训练。

Result: 在三个公开基准和一个外部白血病验证集上，MSA-DCNN在分布偏移和标注稀缺条件下，在准确率、F1和AUC（二分类）指标上优于ViT基线、CNN基线和MICCAI半监督基线，同时使用更少的参数。消融实验验证了各组件的互补贡献。

Conclusion: MSA-DCNN可作为数据高效医学图像分类的实用基础框架，具有推广到结构异质性解剖区域的潜力。

Abstract: Existing deep learning methods perform well in medical image classification but struggle with multi-scale morphology and limited annotations due to fixed sampling and data-hungry training. Existing approaches address these challenges in isolation: DCN-based models provide adaptive sampling but lack explicit multi-scale attention fusion and label-efficient regularisation; multi-scale architectures typically rely on static fusion; and semi-supervised methods target label scarcity without jointly modelling adaptive cross-scale representations. We propose MSA-DCNN, a scale-consistent deformable attention learning framework that introduces adaptive multi-scale sampling, within-scale saliency refinement, learned cross-scale fusion, and auxiliary self-distillation within a unified optimisation scheme, with potential to generalise to structurally heterogeneous anatomy. We evaluate on three public benchmarks and an external hold-out set for leukaemia. MSA-DCNN demonstrates competitive and often better performance against ViT baselines, CNN baselines, and a MICCAI semi-supervised baseline under distribution shift and label scarcity in accuracy, F1, and AUC (binary), while using fewer parameters. Ablations confirm complementary component contributions, supporting MSA-DCNN as a practical foundation for data-efficient medical image classification.

</details>


### [38] [PVCap: Towards Accurate 3D Dense Captioning via PseudoCap and VoxelCapNet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06097)
*Xiaopei Wu,Chenshu Hou,Liang Peng,Dan Xu,Binbin Lin,Xiaoshui Huang,Yuenan Hou,Yu Li,Wenxiao Wang,Haifeng Liu,Deng Cai,Wanli Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PVCap，包含PseudoCap（基于实例级随机混合的数据增强方法）和VoxelCapNet（基于体素特征的密集字幕网络），在ScanRefer和Nr3D基准上分别以CIDEr@0.5IoU超越当前SOTA 11.41%和13.99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D密集字幕描述方法存在两个局限：1）数据增强仅使用全局刚性变换（如旋转），无法改变空间布局，而多样化的空间布局对训练模型描述物体间空间关系至关重要；2）现有工作主要关注字幕生成流水线设计，而骨干网络和检测头等组件使用的网络架构过于简单，难以提取丰富的语义信息。

Method: PVCap由两个核心组件构成：1）PseudoCap：采用实例级随机混合技术，在数据集中对实例进行混合生成具有多样空间布局的伪帧，并通过教师-学生框架为伪帧生成伪字幕标签；2）VoxelCapNet：基于体素特征的鲁棒字幕网络，将字幕头适配到体素级网络架构中，作为3D密集字幕描述的强基线。

Result: 在ScanRefer和Nr3D两个主流基准上，PVCap在CIDEr@0.5IoU指标上分别超越当前SOTA 11.41%和13.99%。

Conclusion: PVCap通过实例级数据增强（PseudoCap）和体素级字幕网络（VoxelCapNet）有效解决了现有3D密集字幕描述方法的局限，显著提升了性能，代码将开源。

Abstract: 3D dense captioning, an emerging vision-language task, aims to generate descriptive sentences for each object in the 3D scene. Despite the impressive results achieved by previous methods, they suffer from two limitations. First, current research often employs global rigid transformations, such as rotation, to augment scenes without changing their spatial layouts. However, diverse spatial layouts are crucial for training a 3D dense captioning model to describe spatial relations between objects. Second, previous works mainly focus on the design of the caption generation pipeline while utilizing a simple network architecture for other components, i.e., backbone and detection head, which is crucial for extracting rich semantic information for captioning. In this paper, we propose PVCap to alleviate the aforementioned problems. Our PVCap consists of PseudoCap and VoxelCapNet. Specifically, PseudoCap employs a random mixing technique on instances within the dataset, generating numerous pseudo frames with diverse spatial layouts at the instance level. By utilizing a teacher-student framework, PseudoCap obtains pseudo caption labels for these pseudo frames. This data augmentation approach significantly increases the number of training samples and enhances the model's ability to describe the environment effectively. Regarding VoxelCapNet, we introduce a robust caption network that utilizes voxel features and adapts the caption head to the voxel-based network architecture. Our VoxelCapNet can serve as a competitive baseline for future research on 3D dense captioning. Extensive experiments are conducted on two prevalent benchmarks, i.e., ScanRefer and Nr3D. Notably, our method surpasses current state-of-the-art by 11.41% and 13.99% in CIDEr@0.5IoU, respectively. Codes will be made publicly available.

</details>


### [39] [RoME: Robust Mixture of Low-Rank Experts against Multiple Adversarial Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06109)
*Woo Jae Kim,Kyle Min,Suhyeon Ha,Joonsung Jeon,Sung-eui Yoon*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoME通过混合低秩专家模型（MoE）解决多扰动对抗训练中的鲁棒性权衡问题，利用双尺度门控和威胁引导的门控多样化机制构建威胁特定模型路径，在联合鲁棒性和自然准确率上超越现有SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多扰动对抗训练（MAT）在应对多种ℓ_p扰动时存在鲁棒性权衡问题，即针对不同威胁的鲁棒性相互制约。

Method: 提出RoME方法：(1) 每个专家作为共享骨干的低秩加性更新，捕捉威胁通用特征，同时专家聚焦威胁特定信息；(2) 双尺度门控利用局部和全局特征中的威胁判别信号；(3) 威胁引导的门控多样化，强制不同威胁下专家利用多样化。

Result: RoME在联合鲁棒性、自然准确率以及对未见威胁的鲁棒性上均优于现有最先进的MAT方法。

Conclusion: RoME通过低秩专家和针对性门控机制有效解决了多扰动对抗训练中的鲁棒性权衡问题。

Abstract: Multi-perturbation adversarial training (MAT) aims to achieve robustness against multiple $\ell_p$ perturbations but suffers from robustness trade-offs between different threats. To address this, we employ a mixture of experts (MoE) to route different threats through distinct model pathways. However, naive application of MoE encounters two critical challenges: experts tend to overlook threat-specific features and redundantly capture features shared across threats, and gating networks suffer from threat-agnostic routing where they learn nearly identical routing patterns across threats, thus preventing the construction of threat-specific model pathways. To this end, we propose Robust Mixture of Low-Rank Experts (RoME), where each expert is a low-rank additive update to the shared backbone, allowing it to capture threat-common features while experts focus on threat-specific information. To address threat-agnostic routing, RoME introduces (i) dual-scale gating that exploits threat-discriminative signals from local and global level features, and (ii) threat-guided gating diversification that enforces diverse expert utilization across threats. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RoME outperforms existing state-of-the-art MAT in union robustness and natural accuracy and improves robustness against unseen threats. Codes are available at https://github.com/wkim97/RoME.

</details>


### [40] [AEGIS: A Mechanism-Guided Defense against Visual Synonym Jailbreaks in Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06120)
*Yuanmin Huang,Zhenfei Zhang,Mi Zhang,Geng Hong,Qinqin He,Jialing Tao,Hui Xue,Min Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AEGIS，一种针对文生图扩散模型中视觉同义词攻击（VSA）的推理时防御方法。通过机制分析发现VSA与显式不安全提示在生成过程中会收敛到稀疏的语义注入注意力头，AEGIS仅在这些脆弱头部施加相似性感知排斥，在保证安全性的同时避免过度抑制良性概念，在多个模型上实现零/极低攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文生图模型的安全对齐方法（输入过滤、结构特征剪枝等）均围绕显式不安全概念进行防御，无法应对视觉同义词攻击（VSA）——即通过看似良性的提示词诱导模型生成违规内容。现有方法面临安全-效用困境：要么无法有效缓解VSA威胁，要么过度抑制视觉相似的良性概念。核心挑战在于VSA在文本层面隐藏不安全目标，仅在生成过程中通过视觉-语义融合才暴露。

Method: 首先通过机制分析发现VSA与显式不安全提示在生成时共享稀疏的语义注入注意力头（semantic-injecting attention heads），这些头部是推理时禁止视觉语义的瓶颈。基于此提出AEGIS自适应防御：仅在这些识别出的脆弱头部上施加相似性感知排斥（similarity-aware repulsion），实现精准干预。

Result: 在16个基线方法对比中，AEGIS在SD 1.4上将领域内暴力/色情VSA的攻击成功率降至0.00/0.03，对领域外显式攻击和对抗攻击的ASR≤0.09。同时保持良性样本保真度，避免抑制硬负例概念，并成功迁移至SD 2.1和FLUX.1。

Conclusion: 从静态压制预定义不安全概念转向动态追踪生成过程中不安全语义的涌现路径，为扩散模型的安全对齐提供了新的机制性视角。AEGIS通过识别并仅干预关键注意力头，有效解决了安全-效用权衡问题。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models have achieved high visual fidelity and broad adoption, but remain vulnerable to safety violations when adversaries exploit them to synthesize illicit content. Existing alignment paradigms, from input sanitization to structural feature pruning, are largely organized around unsafe concepts explicitly exposed during filtering, editing, or localization. This leaves a blind spot for visual synonym attacks (VSA), a jailbreak where benign-looking prompts elicit prohibited imagery through implicit visual associations. As a result, current defenses face a safety-utility dilemma: they may either under-mitigate VSA threats or over-suppress visually similar benign concepts. The core challenge is that VSA hides the unsafe target at the textual surface while revealing it through generation-time visual-semantic convergence. In this work, we therefore shift from static suppression of pre-specified unsafe concepts to dynamic tracing of how unsafe semantics emerge during generation. Our mechanistic analysis shows that VSA and explicit unsafe prompts converge through sparse semantic-injecting attention heads, which serve as inference-time bottlenecks for prohibited visual semantics. Based on this insight, we propose AEGIS (Adaptive Evasion Guard via Identification and Steering), an inference-time defense that applies similarity-aware repulsion only at the identified vulnerable heads. Evaluated against 16 baselines, AEGIS improves both safety and utility. On SD 1.4, it reduces ASR to $\mathbf{0.00}/\mathbf{0.03}$ for in-domain violence/nudity VSA and achieves ASRs $\le \mathbf{0.09}$ on out-of-domain explicit and adversarial attacks. It preserves benign fidelity, avoids suppressing hard-negative concepts, and transfers to SD 2.1 and FLUX.1 after re-identifying the critical heads for each backbone.

</details>


### [41] [RFHNet: Relational and Frequency-Aware Hashing Network for Large-Scale Fine-Grained Food Image Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06148)
*Junsong Wang,Weiqing Min,Guorui Sheng,Tao Yao,Lili Wang,Shuqiang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RFHNet，一种级联分层哈希网络，通过细粒度关系建模、多频率调制融合和分层语义协同三个模块，在细粒度食物图像检索任务上显著超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有哈希方法在细粒度食物场景中表现不佳，难以捕捉微妙的局部语义和频率敏感的视觉线索，需要一种能同时捕获全局结构和细粒度局部细节的方法。

Method: RFHNet包含三个组件：(1) 细粒度关系建模(FRM)捕捉相似食物成分间的细微视觉差异；(2) 多频率调制融合(MFMF)提取信息丰富的多频率特征；(3) 分层语义协同(HSS)自适应整合多层表示并生成判别性哈希码。

Abstract: Fine-grained food image retrieval is a key task in computational gastronomy, with applications in food traceability, dietary monitoring, and smart catering systems. Although hashing-based retrieval is attractive for large-scale search due to its storage efficiency and fast Hamming-distance computation, existing methods often perform poorly in fine-grained food scenarios, where subtle local semantics and frequency-sensitive visual cues are essential. To address this challenge, we propose RFHNet, a cascaded hierarchical hashing network that captures both global structure and fine-grained local details through multi-level representations. RFHNet includes three components: (1) Fine-grained Relation Modeling (FRM) to capture subtle visual differences among similar food components; (2) Multi-Frequency Modulated Fusion (MFMF) to extract informative multi-frequency features; and (3) Hierarchical Semantic Synergy (HSS) to adaptively integrate multi-level representations and generate discriminative hash codes. Experiments on six food-specific benchmarks show that RFHNet consistently outperforms state-of-the-art hashing methods, with mAP gains of 4.44\% to 17.20\% at 12 bits. These results validate the effectiveness of RFHNet for large-scale visual food retrieval and smart catering applications. The source code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [42] [Enhanced Seam Segmentation for Automated Welding Robot in Construction Through Transfer Learning: Addressing Limitations of Bilateral Segmentation Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06150)
*Keonvin Park,Yong Ann Voeurn,Hyeokjun Kweon,Doyun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑自主机器人焊接中，恶劣光照、镜面反射和薄焊缝几何形状常导致分割性能下降，现有方法缺乏针对反射鲁棒性的有效优化。

Method: 以BiSeNetV2为骨干网络，采用迁移学习和混合Cross-Entropy-Lovász损失函数进行学习稳定性导向的优化，而非增加架构复杂度。

Result: Joint IoU达81.76%，mIoU达90.73%；Joint IoU相比OHEM基线提升+22.36个百分点；在反光条件下恢复96.33%的零IoU严重失败案例；FLOPs、参数量和推理速度均保持不变。

Conclusion: 该框架为涉及反射金属表面的机器人焊接提供了一种实用且轻量化的感知方案，尤其适用于轻量级实时分割架构。

Abstract: Reliable seam segmentation is essential for autonomous robotic welding in construction, where harsh illumination, specular reflections, and thin weld geometries often degrade segmentation performance. This study proposes a reflection-robust seam segmentation framework that enhances a BiSeNetV2 backbone through transfer learning and a hybrid Cross-Entropy--Lovász loss. Rather than increasing architectural complexity, the proposed framework improves reflection robustness through learning-stability-oriented optimization. Experimental results show that the proposed method achieves 81.76\% Joint IoU and 90.73\% mIoU, improving Joint IoU by +22.36 percentage points over the OHEM-based baseline while maintaining identical FLOPs, parameter count, and inference speed. The proposed approach also recovers 96.33\% of severe zero-IoU failure cases under reflective conditions. Comparative experiments across BiSeNetV2, DeepLabV3+, UNet, and SegFormer further demonstrate that the proposed optimization strategy is particularly effective for lightweight real-time segmentation architectures. Qualitative analyses additionally show improved seam continuity and reflection robustness in challenging welding environments. These findings suggest that the proposed framework provides a practical and lightweight perception solution for robotic welding applications involving reflective metallic surfaces.

</details>


### [43] [High-Resolution Artwork Outpainting with Global Blueprint Guidance and Layout Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06162)
*Junha Kim,Hyunjoon Park,Donghyeon Cho*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种全局蓝图引导的两阶段扩散框架，用于布局可控的高分辨率图像外扩。第一阶段生成低分辨率全局蓝图（注入边界框条件），第二阶段基于蓝图并行合成高分辨率局部补丁，解决了现有方法结构不稳定、空间可控性差和推理延迟高的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高分辨率图像外扩方法存在三个关键局限：(i) 缺乏可靠的全局规划机制，导致结构不稳定和误差累积；(ii) 空间可控性有限，难以在指定位置放置物体；(iii) 串行补丁生成导致高推理延迟。因此需要一种全局引导、布局可控且高效的并行合成方法。

Method: 提出两阶段扩散框架：Stage 1 利用布局适配器将边界框条件注入Stable Diffusion修补主干，生成低分辨率全局蓝图并提取全局引导特征；Stage 2 利用前向扩散的低频保持特性从蓝图初始化各补丁，并注入蓝图导出的全局引导，实现高分辨率局部补丁的并行合成，消除串行依赖。

Result: 在大规模艺术作品数据集上的实验表明，该方法相比现有基线取得了更优的视觉保真度、更强的语义一致性，并显著减少了推理时间，同时独特地支持显式布局控制。

Conclusion: 提出的全局蓝图引导两阶段扩散框架有效解决了高分辨率图像外扩中的结构稳定性、空间可控性和效率问题，为布局可控的艺术作品外扩提供了新范式。

Abstract: Image outpainting extends an image beyond its original borders, requiring seamless style integration and globally coherent scene completion. Building on the success of diffusion models, recent methods have achieved substantial improvements in visual quality. In practice, however, high-resolution outpainting is commonly performed via progressive expansion around a fixed source image, particularly in artwork scenarios. Despite this progress, existing approaches still suffer from three key limitations: (i) the absence of a reliable global planning mechanism, which leads to structural instability and error accumulation at high resolutions; (ii) limited spatial controllability beyond text prompts, making it difficult to place objects at user-specified locations; and (iii) high inference latency caused by inherently sequential patch generation. To address these issues, we propose a global blueprint-guided two-stage diffusion framework for layout-controllable high-resolution outpainting with efficient parallel synthesis. In Stage 1, we generate a low-resolution global blueprint using a layout adapter that injects bounding-box conditions into a Stable Diffusion inpainting backbone, producing a globally consistent structural plan while extracting global guidance features. In Stage 2, we synthesize high-resolution local patches in parallel by injecting the blueprint-derived global guidance and initializing each patch from the blueprint using the low-frequency preservation property of forward diffusion. This design eliminates sequential dependency while maintaining global coherence. Extensive experiments on large-scale artwork datasets demonstrate improved visual fidelity, stronger semantic consistency, and substantially reduced inference time compared to prior baselines, while uniquely supporting explicit layout control for artwork outpainting.

</details>


### [44] [Revisiting Scene Graph Generation from the Perspective of Detector-Conditioned Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06176)
*Runfeng Qu,Pia K Bideau,Ole Hall,Julie Ouerfelli-Ethier,Klaus Obermayer,Olaf Hellwich*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dual-SGG方法，通过双重查询设计整合检测器基础和查询基础两种场景图生成机制，利用它们互补的预测行为提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检测器基础和查询基础的场景图生成方法存在预测行为差异，但这一差异尚未被系统分析。作者通过受控实验发现两者具有互补线索，进而提出融合方法。

Method: 设计受控实验从检测器条件可达性角度分析预测差异，提出Dual-SGG方法，采用双重查询设计整合基于检测器和基于查询的两种推理机制。

Result: 在Visual Genome、Open Images v6和GQA-200数据集上的大量实验验证了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 检测器基础和查询基础的SGG方法具有互补的预测行为，将两者融合可有效提升场景图生成性能。

Abstract: Scene graph generation (SGG) approaches can be broadly classified into detector-based and query-based methods according to their underlying reasoning mechanisms. However, the discrepancy in their predictive behaviors, induced by these distinct mechanisms, has not been systematically analyzed. In this work, we design a controlled experimental setup to examine prediction discrepancies from the perspective of detector-conditioned reachability. The results suggest clear complementary clues. Motivated by this observation, we introduce a Dual-SGG method that consolidates both reasoning mechanisms via a dual-query design, thereby leveraging the complementary predictive behaviors of both detector-based and query-based methods. Extensive experiments on the Visual Genome, Open Images v6, and GQA-200 datasets demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method.

</details>


### [45] [MoWorld: A Flash World Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06216)
*Team Moxin,Deyi Ji,Tianrun Chen,Xin Zhang,Jiale Yang,Qi Zhu,An Zhao,Zihao Xie,Han Wang,Xuanyi Liu,Yixiang Zhou,Pei Liu,Yi Tan,Cheng Chen,Dayi Zhu,Mingyu Wei,Hanjie Xu,Jun Liao,Siqi Li,Lingyu Lu,Hongye Fang,Hongming Tan,Youjiang Zhu,Taiyu Zhang,Zejian Li,Chaotao Ding,Lanyun Zhu,Yunhe Pan,Lingyun Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MoWorld是一个高效的世界模型框架，通过3D数据引擎、跨帧预训练、蒸馏算法和混合精度并行推理，在NPU上实现高达50 FPS的实时交互，推理成本仅为现有模型的30%-50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型主要关注模型能力扩展，忽视了实用性和推理效率。高帧率推理对于现实世界自动驾驶系统的感知、规划和控制至关重要，但现有方法依赖大规模视频语料库且计算成本高昂，难以大规模部署。

Method: MoWorld采用端到端框架，包含四个关键组件：1) 基于3D视觉和生成建模的可扩展3D原生数据引擎，构建几何一致训练数据；2) 课程式跨帧预训练策略实现稳定可扩展的世界模型学习；3) 高效去噪步长蒸馏算法降低扩散训练成本；4) 混合精度并行推理框架实现低成本实时部署。

Result: MoWorld是首个基于NPU的实时交互世界模型，在NPU上实现高达50 FPS的实时推理，平均推理成本仅为现有世界模型的30%-50%，同时保持电影级视觉质量。

Conclusion: MoWorld通过联合优化模型能力和成本，为世界模型的大规模实际应用提供了实用基础，展示了多种应用场景。

Abstract: The future of World Models depends not only on scaling model capability, but also on scaling practicality and inference efficiency. High-frame-rate inference enables responsive perception, planning, and control in real-world autonomous systems. To this end, we present MoWorld, a cost-effective yet high-performance Flash World Model with an end-to-end framework spanning data generation, pre-training, distillation, and efficient inference, enabling up to 50 FPS real-time interaction with cinematic visual quality without the need of high-end GPUs. To enable large-scale real-world deployment, MoWorld jointly optimizes model capability and cost throughout the entire development pipeline. Specifically, unlike existing approaches that primarily rely on large-scale video corpora, MoWorld is built upon a scalable 3D-native data engine accumulated from our large-scale 3D vision and generative modeling pipeline, enabling the efficient construction of geometrically consistent training data across diverse real-world and synthetic environments. Based on this foundation, a curriculum cross-frame pre-training strategy for stable and scalable World Model learning, an efficient denoising-step distillation algorithm to reduce diffusion training cost, and a mixed-precision parallel inference framework for low-cost real-time deployment. MoWorld is the first real-time interactive World Model built on the Neural Processing Unit (NPU) and can achieves up to 50 FPS in such the devices, enabling practical and efficient deployment at scale. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that MoWorld achieves leading performance; notably, its average inference cost is only 30\%-50\% of that of existing World Models, providing a practical foundation for large-scale real-world applications of World Models. We also demonstrate diverse applications of MoWorld.

</details>


### [46] [WING: A Window-Prior-Based Generative Network with Gated Inception for Cross-Modality CT Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06234)
*Siyuan Mei,Yan Xia,Yipeng Sun,Siming Bayer,Zirong Li,Chengze Ye,Daiqi Liu,Fuxin Fan,Yixing Huang,Andreas Maier*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WING网络，通过将CT强度回归任务分解为多窗口表示，利用Gated Inception Generator生成多窗口预测、Fuse-and-Refine Transformer融合细化、联合对抗训练，在MRI-to-CT和CBCT-to-CT任务上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CT强度具有高动态范围和长尾分布，直接回归会平均化稀疏但临床重要的结构（如骨骼、空气），导致细节丢失。

Method: 1) 将回归目标重构为多个窗口化表示（利用CT强度结构确定且窗口可分离的先验）；2) Gated Inception Generator：多形状核交互生成多窗口预测；3) Fuse-and-Refine Transformer：聚合窗口输出并学习残差细化细节；4) 联合对抗训练增强窗口条件真实性。

Result: 在MRI-to-CT和CBCT-to-CT基准测试上达到SOTA，单模型支持多解剖结构合成。

Conclusion: 窗口化表示有效缓解了CT强度回归中的长尾分布问题，WING以紧凑模型实现了高质量CT生成。

Abstract: Generating CT volumes from MRI and CBCT can improve treatment planning in adaptive radiotherapy while avoiding additional radiation exposure. However, direct regression of CT intensities is challenged by the inherently high dynamic range and long-tailed distributions, thereby averaging out sparse yet clinically important structures. To alleviate this issue, we reformulate the regression target into multiple windowed representations, leveraging the inductive prior that CT intensities are structure-deterministic and window-separable. These windowed views exhibit smoother distributions and admit structured fusion back to the full-range CT. Building on this reformulation, we introduce WING, a WINdow-prior-based Generative network comprising: 1) a new Gated Inception Generator to produce multi-window predictions, enabling multi-shape kernel interactions to capture cross-modality correspondence; 2) a Fuse-and-Refine Transformer to aggregate the windowed outputs and learn residuals for detail refinement; and 3) a joint adversarial training objective to enhance window-conditioned realism. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our compact WING achieves state-of-the-art performance on the MRI-to-CT and CBCT-to-CT benchmarks, while supporting multi-anatomy synthesis with a single model.

</details>


### [47] [VendorBench-100: A Unified Cross-Paradigm Benchmark for Deepfake Image Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06254)
*Sharayu N. Deshmukh,Md Rashidunnabi,Nelton Tiago Gemo,Kurundkar G. D.,Mahamune M. R.,Nilesh K. Deshmukh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VendorBench-100 是一个跨范式的深度伪造图像检测基准，使用100张对抗性图像评估了36个模型（商业API、视觉LLM、开源检测器）。核心发现是商业API中位性能最强，但更重要的是识别出ROC-AUC（排序能力）与MCC（操作点质量）之间存在持续分歧，即强评分区分能力并不一定带来可靠的默认阈值决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度伪造图像检测存在三种根本不同的范式（商业API、零样本视觉语言模型、开源检测器），但它们很少在统一的协议下进行评估，导致直接比较困难。需要一个统一的基准来公平对比这些范式。

Method: 构建VendorBench-100基准：使用100张对抗性图像语料库、统一输出模式和评估框架；采用MCC为主要排名指标、ROC-AUC为阈值无关的排序能力指标；设计了包含8个边缘案例类别的分类法（人脸交换、文本转视频静帧、AI照片编辑、虚拟角色合成、不透明来源图像、压缩研究帧等）。

Result: 商业API的中位性能最强，其次是视觉LLM，最后是开源检测器；但个别开源模型与最佳视觉LLM相当。更重要的是，ROC-AUC与MCC之间存在持续分歧，表明强排序能力不一定带来可靠的默认阈值决策。

Conclusion: 该基准的核心发现不是单一排行榜排名，而是识别出排序能力与操作点质量之间的度量不一致性。强调了在深度伪造检测评估中需要同时关注多个评价指标，而非仅依赖单一指标。

Abstract: Deepfake image detection is currently served by three fundamentally different paradigms: commercial APIs, zero-shot vision-language models (LLMs), and open-source detectors. Despite their widespread use, these paradigms are rarely evaluated under a common protocol, making direct comparison difficult. We introduce VendorBench-100, a cross-paradigm benchmark that evaluates 36 representative models using a single adversarial 100-image corpus, a unified output schema, and a common evaluation framework. To ensure reliable assessment under the corpus's intentional class imbalance, models are ranked primarily by the Matthews correlation coefficient (MCC), with ROC-AUC reported as a threshold-independent measure of ranking ability. Rather than maximizing dataset size, VendorBench-100 emphasizes challenging real-world scenarios through a curated taxonomy of eight edge-case families, including face swaps, text-to-video stills, AI photo edits, avatar compositing, opaque-provenance images, and compressed research frames. Our evaluation shows that commercial APIs achieve the strongest median performance, followed by vision LLMs and open-source detectors. However, individual open-source models remain competitive with the best vision LLMs. More importantly, we identify a consistent divergence between ranking ability (ROC-AUC) and operating-point quality (MCC), demonstrating that strong score discrimination does not necessarily produce reliable default-threshold decisions. This metric disagreement, rather than any single leaderboard ranking, is the central finding of the benchmark. We release the complete evaluation framework and benchmark results to support reproducible future research. The source code and data are available at: https://github.com/sharayu-20/vendorbench-100

</details>


### [48] [Straight-Path Flow Matching for Incomplete Multi-View Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06281)
*Yiteng Yuan,Junyan Wang,Zheyuan Liu,Hong Jia,Lei Fan,Zhulin Tao,Lianbo Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出基于流匹配（Flow Matching）的端到端生成式不完全多视图聚类方法，用确定性ODE流替换扩散模型的随机噪声轨迹，通过线性插值路径在观测视图与缺失视图间建立概率流，并结合簇级对齐和熵对齐实现跨视图聚类一致性，在多个基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散模型的不完全多视图聚类方法虽能生成缺失视图，但扩散过程从簇无关的随机噪声初始化，依赖随机去噪动力学，未针对聚类目标进行显式设计，导致聚类一致性不足。

Method: 提出流匹配框架：1) 在配对视图表示间设计线性插值路径，用确定性ODE流替代扩散的随机轨迹；2) 理论分析证明确定性ODE流在有限步场景下比扩散随机轨迹更优地保持簇一致性；3) 集成直路径流匹配视图补全与簇级对齐、熵对齐机制，实现跨视图聚类一致性。

Result: 在标准不完全多视图聚类基准上取得新的最佳性能。

Conclusion: 确定性ODE流比扩散随机轨迹更适配聚类目标，流匹配框架为生成式不完全多视图聚类提供了更有效的新范式。

Abstract: Incomplete Multi-View Clustering addresses the problem of clustering multi-modal data when certain views are missing. Recent end-to-end generative approaches leverage diffusion models to recover missing views via stochastic noise-to-data trajectories. While expressive, such mechanisms are not explicitly designed for clustering, as they initialize from cluster-agnostic noise and rely on stochastic denoising dynamics. In this work, we revisit probability path design in end-to-end generative IMVC. We introduce a flow-matching framework with a linear interpolation path between paired view representations, that replaces diffusion with probability flows between observed and missing views. We provide a formal analysis showing that deterministic ODE flows are inherently better aligned with clustering objectives than diffusion-based stochastic trajectories, especially in terms of transport mechanisms that respect class-conditional data distributions and maintain cluster consistency in finite-step regimes. Building upon this insight, we develop an end-to-end IMVC architecture that integrates straight-path flow-matching view completion with cluster-level and entropy-based alignment to enforce cross-view clustering consistency. Extensive experiments on standard IMVC benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed framework establishes new state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [49] [Visual graphs for image classification: does the structure affect performance?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06295)
*Alessandra Ibba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了在固定三层GCN架构下不同图构建技术对视觉任务性能的影响，重点关注网络结构如何影响性能以及图利用前的计算阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在视觉任务中表现优异，但无法充分编码图像中的空间、拓扑和语义信息。图神经网络虽能弥补这一不足，但在视觉任务中的有效应用尚未被充分探索，且现有研究视角有限。

Method: 在固定的三层GCN架构下，系统比较当前多种图构建技术，通过实证研究分析网络结构对性能的影响。

Result: 网络结构显著影响模型性能；图利用前的计算阶段（即图构建阶段）受结构本身的强烈影响，是重要的方法论贡献。

Conclusion: 图构建技术及网络结构的选择对GCN在视觉任务中的表现至关重要，后续研究应关注图构建阶段对整体性能的影响。

Abstract: Deep learning models have emerged in machine learning and related fields, demonstrating astonishing performance in various visual tasks. Despite their great success, however, these models are unable to fully encode intrinsic visual structures, and often ignore the spatial, topological, and semantic information contained within an image. Graph neural networks offer a good framework to face this aspect, but their effective use for visual tasks has been only partly explored and mainly starting from a limited perspective. This work aims to address this gap by conducting a systematic comparison of current graph construction techniques within the context of a fixed three-layer GCN architecture. Through an empirical study, it demonstrates in particular how the network structure affects performance and provides an important methodological contribution regarding the computational stages preceding graph utilization, which will be strongly influenced by the structure itself.

</details>


### [50] [Synthetic-to-Real Translation for Class-Agnostic Motion Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06319)
*Yizheng Wu,Hongwei Fan,Kewei Wang,Ruibo Li,Xingyi Li,Xiao Song,Zhe Wang,Chenjing Ding,Dongliang Wang,Zhiguo Cao,Guosheng Lin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向合成到真实迁移的运动预测框架（SRMP），通过引入物体感知运动预测和物体辅助运动增强两个关键组件来解决域偏移问题，并构建了首个专为SRMP研究的合成4D LiDAR数据集Motion4D，实验证明该方法能有效弥合域间隙并在真实场景中取得优异性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实世界中运动标注成本高昂，因此希望利用合成数据迁移运动知识到真实数据，但现有的朴素运动回归方法对合成-真实域偏移十分敏感，导致知识迁移不可靠。

Method: 提出运动知识迁移框架，包含：(1) 物体感知运动预测——显式建模运动模式与物体先验的联合分布以学习域不变特征；(2) 物体辅助运动增强——利用学习到的物体先验过滤运动噪声的运动标签精炼机制。此外，构建了首个面向SRMP研究的合成4D LiDAR数据集Motion4D。

Result: 实验表明该方法有效弥合了域间隙，在真实场景上取得了优于基线方法的性能。

Conclusion: 所提框架能够可靠地实现合成到真实的运动知识迁移，为降低自动驾驶运动预测对真实标注的依赖提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Motion understanding is critical for ensuring safety and robustness in autonomous driving systems, driving increasing interest in motion prediction. A key challenge in this domain is the high cost associated with acquiring real-world motion labels. It is therefore ideal if we could transfer motion knowledge from synthetic data to real data. In this context, we explore the potential of synthetic-to-real translation for motion prediction (SRMP). However, the most used naive motion regression methods are notably sensitive to the synthetic-to-real domain shift, resulting in unreliable knowledge translation. To address this, we propose a novel approach integrating a motion knowledge translation framework with two key components: (1) objectness-aware motion prediction, which explicitly models the joint distribution of motion patterns and objectness priors to improve domain-invariant feature learning, and (2) objectness-aided motion enhancement, a motion label refinement mechanism that leverages learned objectness priors to filter motion noise. Furthermore, we present a physically-based pipeline for generating Motion4D, the first synthetic 4D LiDAR dataset tailored for SRMP research, addressing the lack of synthetic motion datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that our approach effectively bridges the domain gaps and yields superior performance on real scenes.

</details>


### [51] [Generalized Synthetic Image Detection with Enhanced RGB-Noise Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06354)
*Zhen Li,Gang Cao,Tian Zhang,Lifang Yu,Shaowei Weng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出RNSIDNet，一种通过增强RGB-噪声表征学习实现鲁棒泛化深度伪造图像检测的双分支框架，结合CLIP注意力增强的RGB分支与Bayar卷积的噪声分支，并引入硬样本感知对比学习策略，在八个基准数据集上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有伪造检测网络因依赖单域表征和传统二分类优化，在跨模型泛化和真实世界退化场景下表现不佳，亟需一种更具鲁棒性和泛化能力的检测方法。

Method: 提出RNSIDNet双分支架构：1) RGB分支利用注意力增强的CLIP骨干提取全局语义特征；2) 噪声分支通过Bayar卷积捕获高频噪声伪影；3) 采用特征级线性调制(FiLM)模块实现RGB语义对噪声特征的动态调制；4) 设计硬样本感知对比学习(HSCL)策略，通过惩罚难样本来最大化真实与合成域之间的判别边界。

Result: 在八个公开基准数据集上的大量实验表明，该模型在泛化能力、鲁棒性和计算效率方面均达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: RNSIDNet通过多模态表征融合与对比学习优化，有效解决了跨模型泛化和真实退化场景下的伪造检测难题，为通用合成图像检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of large-scale generative models has accelerated the spread of highly deceptive AI-generated images, making generalized synthetic image detection a critical imperative. Existing forensic networks often struggle with cross-model generalization and realworld degradations due to their reliance on single-domain representations and conventional binary classification optimization. To overcome these limitations, we propose RNSIDNet, a novel forensic framework that achieves robust detection through enhanced RGB-Noise representation learning. Specifically, our method employs a dual-branch architecture where global RGB semantics, extracted by an attention-refined CLIP backbone, dynamically modulate highfrequency noise artifacts captured by Bayar convolutions via a Feature-wise Linear Modulation (FiLM) module. To further enhance the learned representations, we design a Hard Sample-aware Contrastive Learning (HSCL) strategy. By explicitly penalizing challenging training samples, HSCL reshapes the latent feature space to maximize the discriminative margin between pristine and synthetic domains. Extensive experiments across eight public benchmark datasets verify that our model achieves state-of-the-art performance, delivering superior generalization ability, robustness, and computational efficiency. Code and dataset will be publicly available on https://github.com/multimediaFor/RNSIDNet.

</details>


### [52] [VaseMuseum: Digital Intelligent Museum for Ancient Greek Pottery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06374)
*Jiazi Wang,Nonghai Zhang,Qiushi Xie,Zeyu Zhang,Yufeng Chen,Yang Zhao,Ling Shao,Hao Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VaseMuseum，一个轻量模块化的多模态智能体框架，用于古希腊陶器数字博物馆，通过源级和响应级控制机制结合GRPO风格选择，增强VLMs在文化遗产领域的可靠性和不确定性校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在文化遗产领域（如古希腊陶器），开放式的文物解读需要将细粒度2D/3D视觉证据与专业策展知识结合，但检索过程可能引入弱来源和不可验证的引用；同时，当证据不完整、有噪声或模糊时，VLMs常给出自信但无依据的回答而非校准的不确定性。

Method: VaseMuseum包含交互式虚拟博物馆和VaseAgent。VaseAgent支持多模态感知、3D感知推理、外部知识检索和推理时可靠性控制：源级控制从权威来源检索并选择多样可验证证据；响应级控制将生成声明与证据池核对，鼓励中立、有证据边界的回答；采用无训练的GRPO风格选择机制，在保留VLM主干的情况下偏好具有有效引用和校准置信度的响应。

Result: 在真实数字博物馆模拟实验中，VaseMuseum相比启用搜索的VLM基线，在知识密集型查询上提高了引用有效性，减少了幻觉，并在模糊情况下生成更中立的回答。

Conclusion: VaseMuseum通过模块化的可靠性控制机制有效解决了VLMs在文化遗产开放域问答中的证据引用和不确定性校准问题，为数字博物馆中的可信AI辅助探索提供了可行的轻量化方案。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have made interactive digital museums increasingly feasible by connecting 3D digitization with natural-language artifact exploration. However, in cultural heritage domains such as ancient Greek pottery, reliable VLM assistance is limited by two challenges. First, open-ended interpretation requires grounding fine-grained 2D/3D visual evidence in specialized curatorial knowledge, yet the retrieval process may introduce weak sources and unverifiable references. Second, when the available evidence is incomplete, noisy, or ambiguous, VLMs often produce confident but unsupported answers instead of calibrated uncertainty. To address these challenges, we propose VaseMuseum, a lightweight and modular multimodal agent framework for intelligent digital museums of ancient Greek pottery. VaseMuseum combines an interactive virtual museum with VaseAgent, which supports both 2D images and 3D artifacts through multimodal perception, 3D-aware reasoning, external knowledge retrieval, and inference-time reliability control. Specifically, VaseAgent retrieves evidence from authoritative web and museum knowledge sources, and source-level control selects diverse and verifiable evidence before generation. Meanwhile, response-level control checks generated claims against the evidence pool and encourages neutral, evidence-bounded answers when support is insufficient or conflicting. Moreover, a training-free GRPO-style selection mechanism favors responses with valid references and calibrated confidence without updating the VLM backbone. Experiments in a realistic digital museum simulation show that VaseMuseum improves citation validity, reduces hallucinations on knowledge-intensive queries, and produces more neutral answers under ambiguity compared with search-enabled VLM baselines.

</details>


### [53] [What Images Cannot Say: Language-Guided Olfactory Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06402)
*Eleftherios Tsonis,Xi Wang,Vicky Kalogeiton*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SCENT是一个多模态框架，利用语言作为视觉与嗅觉之间的语义桥梁，通过视觉-语言模型生成场景描述来引导嗅觉表示学习，在纽约气味数据集上实现了跨模态检索的最先进性能，并能分解复杂气味混合物。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图像能展示场景外观但难以传达气味感受，且许多嗅觉线索来自不可直接见的上下文环境因素，现有数据集难以对齐气味信号与图像。

Method: 使用视觉-语言模型生成包含物体、环境上下文和可嗅觉线索的场景描述；训练嗅觉编码器将电子鼻信号映射到与视觉和文本表示对齐的共享嵌入空间；引入语言引导的潜在分解来分离物体特定气味与环境上下文贡献。

Result: 在New York Smells数据集上，SCENT在气味到图像和气味到文本检索任务中显著优于纯视觉基线方法，达到最先进性能，并能实现复杂气味混合物的解耦。

Conclusion: 上下文语义信息对于在多模态学习中锚定嗅觉感知至关重要，该框架为未来相关研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Images tell us what a scene looks like, but rarely what it would feel like to be there. While recent datasets pair visual scenes with electronic-nose measurements, aligning smell signals with images remains challenging because many olfactory cues arise from contextual environmental factors that are not directly visible in pixels. We introduce SCENT, a multimodal framework that uses language guidance as a semantic bridge between vision and olfaction. Our approach leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) to generate scene descriptors capturing objects, environmental context, and plausible ambient smell cues suggested by the visual scene. These descriptors provide semantic guidance for learning olfactory representations. We train a smell encoder that maps electronic-nose signals into a shared embedding space aligned with both visual and textual representations, and introduce a languageguided latent decomposition that separates object-specific odors from contextual environmental contributions. Experiments on the New York Smells dataset demonstrate that SCENT significantly improves crossmodal retrieval compared to vision-only baselines, achieving state-of-theart performance on smell-to-image and smell-to-text retrieval tasks. In addition, our framework produces interpretable olfactory representations that enable the disentanglement of complex smell mixtures. Our results reveal the importance of contextual semantic information for grounding olfactory perception in multimodal learning and pave the way for future research in this area.

</details>


### [54] [HoloCount: A Holistic Visual Counting Benchmark for MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06420)
*Jinhong Deng,Limeng Qiao,Guanglu Wan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: HoloCount是一个基于三级层次分类法的全面视觉计数基准，用于评估多模态大语言模型在语义计数、分析计数和鲁棒性测试三大维度上的表现。对20多个SOTA模型的评估显示，从感知到复杂推理和对抗场景，顶尖模型的性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉计数基准过于简化，未能捕捉MLLMs在逻辑约束或对抗条件下出现的复杂失效模式，且MLLMs在定量精度上存在显著瓶颈（数值幻觉），缺乏系统性诊断框架。

Method: 提出HoloCount基准，构建三级层次分类法：(1) 语义计数（原子及属性枚举）；(2) 分析计数（空间与集合推理的逻辑组合）；(3) 鲁棒性测试（高密度场景、语言偏见等对抗性条件）。对20余种SOTA MLLMs进行全面评估。

Result: 顶尖MLLMs在从感知任务转向复杂分析推理和对抗场景时性能显著下降，揭示了当前模型在定量计数能力上的系统性缺陷。

Conclusion: HoloCount系统揭示了MLLMs计数能力的现状与差距，为开发更可靠、更扎实的多模态系统提供了路线图。

Abstract: Visual counting is a fundamental pillar of multimodal intelligence, requiring a seamless integration of fine-grained grounding and spatial reasoning. While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in qualitative scene understanding, their quantitative precision remains a significant bottleneck, often characterized by persistent numerical hallucinations. Existing counting benchmarks primarily focus on basic perception in simplified contexts, failing to capture the complex failure modes that emerge under logical constraints or adversarial conditions. To address these limitations, we introduce HoloCount, a holistic and diagnostically rich benchmark structured around a three-level hierarchical taxonomy. HoloCount evaluates MLLMs across: (1) Semantic Counting, focusing on atomic and property-based enumeration; (2) Analytical Counting, assessing logical composition through spatial and set-based reasoning; and (3) Robustness Testing, probing model integrity against adverse scenarios and grounded counter-priors, such as high-density scenes and linguistic biases. Through an exhaustive evaluation of over 20 state-of-the-art MLLMs, we reveal a critical performance gap: even top-tier models degrade significantly as tasks transition from perception to complex analytical reasoning and adverse scenarios. Our findings provide a systematic landscape of current MLLM counting capabilities and offer a roadmap for developing more grounded and reliable multimodal systems. The dataset is available at https://mm-mvr.github.io/HoloCount/.

</details>


### [55] [PIPBench: A Profile-Inclusive Framework for Personalized Image Generation Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06440)
*Yuhang Wu,Shuxiang Zhang,Wee Hian Ching,Chi Zhang,Miao Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个包含用户画像的个性化图像生成基准PIPBench，并构建了基于心理学和人口统计学维度的数据收集与生成流程，实验揭示了现有方法在实现个性化文本到图像合成方面的关键局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到图像模型（如DALLE-3）能遵循多样化提示，但无法适应个体审美偏好。需要一种能够根据用户历史偏好图像和简短提示来对齐输出的个性化图像生成方法，并建立相应的评估基准。

Method: 提出PIPBench基准，构建了基于心理学和人口统计学维度的数据构建流程，包括真实用户数据收集和可扩展的基于智能体的数据生成。在该基准上对代表性方法进行了全面评估。

Result: 实验揭示了现有方法在个性化图像生成方面的关键局限性。

Conclusion: 现有方法在个性化文本到图像合成方面存在不足，这为未来的研究提出了新的挑战和机遇。

Abstract: Recent text-to-image models such as DALLE-3 excel at following diverse prompts yet remain blind to individual aesthetic preferences. We study personalized image generation, where models must align outputs with a user's implicit visual preferences based on a few historically preferred images and a short prompt. To this end, we introduce PIPBench, the first profile-inclusive benchmark for evaluating personalized image generation. We further propose a novel data construction pipeline that leverages psychological and demographic profiling dimensions for both real-user data collection and scalable agent-based data generation. Using PIPBench, we conduct a thorough evaluation of representative line of methods. Our experiments reveal key limitations in existing methods, suggesting new challenges and opportunities for personalized text-to-image synthesis. Project page: https://wuyuhang05.github.io/PIPBench/

</details>


### [56] [Andha-Dhun: A First Look at Audio Descriptions in Hindi](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06457)
*Ritabrata Chakraborty,Divy Kala,Nisheeth Bhooshan Gupta,Ganji Sreeram,Pailla Balakrishna Reddy,Makarand Tapaswi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究印地语音频描述（ADs），构建了首个印地语AD数据集Andha-Dhun，探索了两种生成方法（直接生成和翻译），并发现服务于印度BLV受众的AD更需要内容适应性而非严格忠实于源语言。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着印度CBFC强制要求提供音频描述，印地语AD需求迫切，但目前尚无任何针对印度语言的AD生成研究工作。

Method: 构建Andha-Dhun数据集（8部电影的印地语人工AD），探索两种方案：（i）从英文密集视频描述直接生成印地语AD；（ii）将英文AD翻译为印地语。使用困惑度和LLM作为裁判评估流畅度和质量。

Result: 机器翻译会引入伪影并缩小多样性；直接机器翻译无法适应文化参照；人工翻译虽优于机器翻译但仍不及原始印地语AD。

Conclusion: 印地语AD的核心目标是为印度BLV受众提供无障碍服务，这要求内容适应性优先于对源语言的严格忠实。

Abstract: Audio Descriptions (ADs) narrate visual content for Blind and Low Vision (BLV) audiences during gaps in audiovisual media. There is growing momentum around ADs in movies and TV shows, and with mandates from India's Central Board of Film Certification (CBFC), there is a need to expand ADs beyond English. Yet, there is no work that generates ADs for any Indian language. To address this gap, we present the first systematic study of ADs in Hindi, contributing to aspects such as data, generation, and evaluation. We introduce Andha-Dhun, the first dataset of human-authored Hindi ADs collected from 8 full-length movies. We explore two approaches for generating ADs in Hindi: (i) directly from English dense video descriptions, and (ii) translating English ADs into Hindi. We evaluate these approaches using perplexity and LLM-as-a-judge metrics to assess fluency and quality respectively. We also analyze movies that have both English and Hindi human-authored ADs and find that naive translation introduces artifacts and narrows diversity compared to original Hindi ADs. Direct machine translation fails to adapt cultural references, while human-translated ADs do better but still fall short. Our findings emphasize that the purpose of Hindi ADs is accessibility for Indian BLV audiences, and that this requires adapting content for the audience more than strict fidelity to the source.

</details>


### [57] [Verification of Dynamic Holographic Behavior in Identity Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06466)
*Glen Pouliquen,Joseph Chazalon,Guillaume Chiron,Thierry Géraud,Ahmad Montaser Awal*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文针对身份证件上光学可变器件（全息图）的远程真伪验证问题，提出了三个贡献：1）新公开数据集MIDV-DynAttack，扩展了现有MIDV-Holo数据集，增加了真实静态和动态攻击样本；2）一种新颖的验证方法，通过分析动态行为和外观来评估全息图真实性，且无需动态攻击样本即可训练，达到了新最优性能；3）对现有方法的基准测试，展示了它们无法应对动态攻击和新的挑战性攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要针对静态伪造（如纸张复印）进行验证，尚未评估其对真实动态攻击（如手工制作全息图）的检测能力，且缺乏公共数据集。此外，现有方法通常只能检测已知攻击类型，难以泛化到未知的新型攻击。

Method: 1）构建了MIDV-DynAttack数据集，包含真实静态和动态攻击样本；2）提出一种新颖的验证方法，通过分析全息图的动态行为和外观特征进行真伪判别，训练时无需动态攻击样本；3）建立明确的评估协议，对现有方法进行基准测试。

Result: 新方法在动态攻击检测上达到了新最优性能，而现有方法在应对动态攻击时表现不足。

Conclusion: 该工作有效弥补了动态全息图攻击检测的研究空白，为自动化全息图验证提供了数据基础和方法支撑。

Abstract: This paper addresses the remote verification of the authenticity of Optically Variable Devices (commonly known as holograms) on identity documents. Typically placed over the cardholder's photo, these devices provide strong and easily verifiable security for human inspection but pose challenges for automated verification. Existing approaches easily cover static frauds (e.g. paper photocopy) and can be evaluated for such, but their capacity to detect real, dynamic fraud cases (e.g. handcrafted hologram) has not been evaluated to date because of the lack of public datasets. Furthermore, they are usually trained to detect known attack types, and few of them can generalize to new, unseen attacks. This work features three contributions to address these limitations: 1) a new public dataset, MIDV-DynAttack, which extends the existing MIDV-Holo dataset with realistic, static and dynamic attacks against identity document specimens, tripling the number of attack samples compared to the original dataset, 2) a novel verification method which can assess the authenticity of a specific hologram thanks to the analysis of its dynamic behavior and appearance, can be trained without dynamic attack samples, and exhibits new state-of-the-art performance, 3) a benchmark of existing approaches which follows a clear evaluation protocol and emphasizes the inability of other approaches to deal with dynamic attacks, as well as new challenging attacks to deal with. Code and dataset are publicly available at https://github.com/EPITAResearchLab/pouliquen.25.icdar.

</details>


### [58] [EgoPolice: A Benchmark for Egocentric Video Understanding in High-Stakes Police Body-Worn Camera Footage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06468)
*Max Gonzalez Saez-Diez,Jihoon Chung,Adam D. Wolsky,Gregory Lanzalotto,Dean Knox,Jonathan Mummolo,Brandon M. Stewart,Olga Russakovsky*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EgoPolice是一个来自真实执法记录仪视频的、以自我为中心视角的警察-平民互动数据集，提供了细粒度的行为标注，并作为评估视频模型在高风险、运动剧烈场景下感知能力的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自我中心视频数据集缺乏对警察-平民互动这一关键社会场景的细粒度标注，无法支持执法行为研究和模型在该场景下的鲁棒性评估。

Method: 从公开的执法记录仪视频中筛选并逐秒标注警察-平民互动行为标签，构建包含剧烈运动、密集人际交互和罕见高风险事件的数据集，设计分类和多选问答两类任务。

Result: 即使是最先进的视频模型（如Gemini 2.5 Pro）在预测"武器出鞘"等高危动作时仍表现不佳，表明当前模型在运动鲁棒性和上下文感知方面存在明显不足。

Conclusion: EgoPolice不仅可作为更具挑战性的自我中心视频感知基准，还为开发能够从大规模执法记录仪视频库中自动识别关键事件的模型提供了基础，有助于提升下游人工审查效率。

Abstract: We introduce EgoPolice, a carefully curated dataset of real, egocentric police-civilian interactions, sourced from publicly available body-worn camera videos. We select police-civilian action labels that are critical for police behavioral research and annotate them at a second-by-second granularity. The videos feature rapid and irregular camera motion, dense human interactions, and rare high-stakes events, making the dataset a challenging benchmark for motion-robust and context-aware egocentric perception. We provide two different tasks, classification and multiple-choice question-answering, and benchmark both open-source and closed-source models. We find that even the best video models like Gemini 2.5 Pro still struggle to accurately predict high-risk actions such as "Weapon Out". Beyond serving as a benchmark, EgoPolice provides a foundation for developing models capable of identifying events of interest in large-scale body-worn camera video repositories, enabling more efficient downstream human review.

</details>


### [59] [A VLM-Enhanced Framework for Comprehensive Traffic Sign Condition Assessment Integrating Daytime Visual Performance and Nighttime Retroreflectivity Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06478)
*Linlin Zhang,Neema Jakisa Owor,Xiang Yu,Abby Watts,Yaw Adu-Gyamfi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合日间视觉语言模型评估与夜间LiDAR逆反射测量的交通标志综合检测框架，利用微调后的VLM进行日间四项指标评分，通过CLIP和情感分析转化为数值，结合夜间逆反射数据构建标志状况指数，实现比传统人工检测更经济高效的替代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统交通标志检测依赖人工巡检（主观、劳动密集、存在安全隐患）或昂贵的逆反射仪（小机构难以承担），且现有研究多聚焦日间或夜间单一维度，缺乏将两者整合的综合评估方法。

Method: 使用三个微调后的视觉语言模型（LLaVA、Qwen、InternVL）评估日间四项关键因素（可读性、颜色、表面与形状完整性、周围环境条件），通过情感分析和CLIP评分将VLM预测转化为数值分数；夜间性能通过LiDAR逆反射测量及校准流程评估；最终将二者整合为标志状况指数用于维护决策。

Result: LLaVA和Qwen优于InternVL，在所有因素上达到0.67-0.76的双向余弦相似度。在462个验证标志中，68个因逆反射性能不足被标记为需要立即更换。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了一种成本效益高的替代方案，可替代传统人工巡检，实现全面的交通标志状况评估。

Abstract: Traffic signs are crucial components of road safety, serving as visual tools under all lighting conditions. The Manual on Uniform Traffic Control Devices (MUTCD) specifies daytime visual factors such as legibility and color contrast, and nighttime retroreflectivity requirements. Traditional assessment methods rely on manual inspections, which the Federal Highway Administration (FHWA) notes are subjective, labor-intensive and pose safety concerns, while retroreflectometers are expensive and unaffordable for smaller agencies. Most existing studies focus on either daytime factors or nighttime retroreflectivity but rarely integrate both aspects comprehensively. This study develops a novel framework that systematically evaluates traffic signs through integrated daytime-nighttime assessment. The methodology employs three fine-tuned Vision Language Models (VLMs) for daytime visual performance assessment across four key factors: legibility, color, surface and shape integrity, and surrounding environment conditions. VLM predictions are converted to numerical scores through sentiment analysis and Contrastive Language-Image Pre-Training (CLIP) scoring, while nighttime performance is assessed using LiDAR-derived retroreflectivity following established calibration procedures. The framework integrates these components into a comprehensive Sign Condition Index (SCI) for maintenance guidance. Evaluation results demonstrated that LLaVA and Qwen outperformed InternVL, achieving bidirectional cosine similarity scores of 0.67-0.76 across all factors. Among 462 validated traffic signs, 68 were flagged by the proposed framework as requiring immediate replacement due to inadequate retroreflectivity performance. This research provides a cost-effective alternative to traditional manual inspections for comprehensive traffic sign condition assessment.

</details>


### [60] [Prompt-Adapter Context Routing for Parameter-Efficient Multi-Shot Long Video Extrapolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06481)
*Anna Córdoba,Adam Puente Tercero,Nerea Angulo Hijo,Mar Linares Tercero,Julia Barrientos,Ainhoa Miranda,Jesús Olivera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PACR-Video是一种参数高效的多镜头长视频外推框架，通过冻结文本到视频扩散变压器并添加低秩时间适配器和递归提示库，在无需全生成器微调的情况下保持了实体、场景、风格和因果进程的一致性，在多项基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长视频生成方法在保持跨镜头的实体一致性、场景结构、视觉风格和因果推进方面存在困难，且全模型微调计算成本过高，亟需一种参数高效且能保持长时间连贯性的解决方案。

Method: 冻结文本到视频扩散变压器，添加由学习到的镜头角色提示令牌条件化的低秩时间适配器；构建递归提示库存储先前镜头的实体、位置、动作和风格提示，通过适配器门控根据预测的叙事依赖进行路由；采用镜头局部/故事全局的联合优化目标（下一镜头重建、跨镜头身份对比、提示稀疏正则化）和适配器组合调度策略。

Result: 在六个多镜头和长视频基准上，PACR-Video在分布质量、语义对齐、身份一致性、时间平滑性、运动稳定性、过渡连贯性和人类偏好方面全面超越文本到视频、调优、记忆增强、流式和递归上下文基线方法。

Conclusion: 紧凑的提示路由和轻量级时间适配能够提供足够可控的能力来实现稳定的长视频外推，验证了参数高效策略在多镜头长视频生成中的有效性。

Abstract: We present PACR-Video, a parameter-efficient framework for multi-shot long video extrapolation that preserves recurring entities, scene structure, visual style, and causal progression without full generator fine-tuning. PACR-Video keeps a text-to-video diffusion transformer frozen and augments it with low-rank temporal adapters conditioned by learned shot-role prompt tokens. To maintain long-horizon coherence, it builds a recursive prompt bank that stores compact entity, location, action, and style prompts from previous shots, then routes them through adapter gates according to predicted narrative dependencies. A Shot-Local/Story-Global tuning objective combines next-shot reconstruction, cross-shot identity contrast, and prompt sparsity regularization, while an adapter composition schedule balances early-shot visual consistency with later-shot event progression and viewpoint change. Across six multi-shot and long-video benchmarks, PACR-Video outperforms text-to-video, tuning-based, memory-augmented, streaming, and recursive-context baselines on distributional quality, semantic alignment, identity consistency, temporal smoothness, motion stability, transition coherence, and human preference. These results show that compact prompt routing and lightweight temporal adaptation provide sufficient controllable capacity for stable long video extrapolation.

</details>


### [61] [AirflowAttack: Thermal-Airflow Adversarial Perturbations against Infrared Remote-Sensing Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06485)
*Cong Su,Jiaju Han,Xuemeng Sun,Chengyin Hu,Qike Zhang,Jiujiang Guo,Yiwei Wei,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出首个针对红外遥感视觉语言模型（VLM）的对抗攻击方法AirflowAttack，利用热气流湍流作为扰动先验，在五个CLIP骨干上平均零样本场景分类攻击成功率达48.5%，显著优于四种红外物理基线（27.7-37.0%），并揭示了红外VLM生态系统的关键脆弱性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs日益部署于红外遥感图像的安全关键场景，但其对抗鲁棒性尚未被研究。

Method: 轻量级生成器合成单一、与输入无关的扰动，并通过物理上合理的气流模式正则化约束；在单一替代CLIP模型上优化后迁移攻击其他模型。

Result: 平均零样本场景分类ASR达48.5%（五个CLIP骨干），远超四种红外特定物理基线（27.7-37.0%）；对六个最先进VLM使场景分类准确率相对下降高达38.2%；部分模型反而对扰动更自信，将其误认为温度梯度、对流等真实热证据。

Conclusion: AirflowAttack暴露了红外VLM生态系统的关键脆弱性，且气流先验在不牺牲攻击成功率的前提下显著提升了物理合理性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed on infrared (IR) remote sensing imagery in security-critical settings, yet their adversarial robustness remains unexamined. We present AirflowAttack, to our knowledge the first adversarial attack for IR remote-sensing VLMs and the first to weaponize thermal-airflow turbulence as the perturbation prior. A lightweight generator synthesizes a single input-agnostic perturbation regularized toward physically plausible airflow patterns. Optimized on one surrogate CLIP model, it attains a mean zero-shot scene-classification attack success rate (ASR, the fraction of samples whose top-1 class changes) of 48.5% across five diverse CLIP backbones, far exceeding four IR-specific physical baselines (27.7--37.0%). Applied to six state-of-the-art VLMs, it cuts scene-classification accuracy by up to 38.2% relative, yet paradoxically makes some models more confident in their IR analysis, confabulating the perturbation as genuine thermal evidence such as temperature gradients and convection. Ablations show the airflow prior raises physical plausibility at no measurable cost to attack success. Together with a benchmark spanning eleven models and four tasks, these findings expose critical vulnerabilities in the rapidly expanding IR VLM ecosystem.

</details>


### [62] [CAIRN: Cross-Room 3D Scene Understanding with Topology-Aware Large Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06534)
*He Liang,Chenyang Ma,Yiming Zhang,Sangyun Shin,Andrew Markham,Niki Trigoni,Yuhang He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CAIRN是一种拓扑感知的3D大语言模型，专为多房间3D场景理解设计。它通过对齐Transformer注意力与场景层次结构，引入图神经网络增强对象token、房间token以及层次化注意力掩码，显著超越了先前3D-LLMs在多房间基准CAIRN-MR上的表现，同时在单房间基准上保持竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D-LLMs仅能处理简化的单房间场景，缺乏对真实世界多房间互联环境（包含多个连通房间和多样化物体类别）的推理能力。

Method: CAIRN包含三个核心设计：(1) 通过图神经网络为对象token注入房间局部关系上下文；(2) 引入可学习的房间token实现房间级抽象；(3) 应用具有几何偏置的层次化注意力掩码，按照场景拓扑结构路由信息，使Transformer注意力与场景层次结构对齐。

Result: CAIRN在CAIRN-MR基准的所有任务（包括定位、描述和四种问答任务）上大幅超越先前3D-LLMs，同时在五个单房间基准上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 显式建模场景拓扑层次结构（对象级关系和房间级连通性）能显著提升3D-LLMs在多房间复杂环境中的理解与推理能力，且该方法不会牺牲在传统单房间任务上的表现。

Abstract: Existing 3D scene-grounded Large Language Models (3D-LLMs) focus on answering questions grounded in simplified single-room 3D scenes, lacking the ability to reason over real-world household environments containing multiple interconnected rooms and diverse object categories. We introduce CAIRN, a topology-aware 3D-LLM for multi-room 3D scene understanding. CAIRN aligns transformer attention with scene hierarchy, giving the model explicit awareness of object-level relations and room-level connectivity. It enriches object tokens with room-local relational context via a graph neural network, introduces learned room tokens for room-level abstraction, and applies a hierarchical attention mask with geometric bias to route information according to scene topology. CAIRN is developed on CAIRN-MR, a benchmark we introduce on HM3D for multi-room 3D scene understanding, covering grounding, captioning, and four question-answering tasks that progressively evaluate from intra-room perception to cross-room reasoning. Experiments show that CAIRN outperforms prior 3D-LLMs by a large margin across all CAIRN-MR tasks while remaining competitive on five single-room benchmarks.

</details>


### [63] [MonoIR-RS: Infrared Remote Sensing Vision-Language Learning with CLIP and VLM Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06552)
*Jiaju Han,Ma Yaqi,Yahui Chai,Xuemeng Sun,Xin Li,Qike Zhang,Yingying Zhao,Xiang Chen,Luwei Yang,Chengyin Hu,Jiahuan Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MonoIR-RS是一个大规模红外遥感视觉-语言数据集与基准，通过CLIP对比适应和VLM指令微调，将红外遥感图像与语言对齐，在多个模型上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感视觉-语言资源和模型主要聚焦可见光波段，红外遥感视觉-语言理解尚未被充分探索。

Method: 从FusionRS相同源池构建MonoIR-RS数据集，包含60万张合成红外图像和59,032条红外感知标注记录；采用CLIP风格对比适应和VLM指令微调；标注从灰度结构和红外风格对比角度重写。

Result: 合成红外图像在AVIID基准上比灰度转换更接近真实热成像；IR感知适应使CLIP平均召回率提升12.8个百分点；VLM字幕红外线索覆盖率达100%，RGB颜色泄漏降至接近零。

Conclusion: MonoIR-RS提供了可控、可复现的红外遥感视觉-语言对齐测试平台，将红外模态从RGB-IR双模态学习中解耦出来。

Abstract: Infrared remote-sensing imagery captures intensity structure, object-background contrast, and illumination-invariant cues often invisible in RGB imagery. Yet, most remote-sensing vision-language resources and models focus on visible-band semantics, leaving infrared vision-language understanding underexplored. We introduce MonoIR-RS, a large-scale infrared remote-sensing vision-language dataset and benchmark that couples IR-aware data construction with CLIP-style contrastive adaptation and VLM instruction tuning. Built from the same source pool and split as FusionRS, MonoIR-RS retains the infrared image as the model-facing modality, yielding 600,000 synthesized infrared images and 59,032 retained IR-aware caption records. The model experiments use this retained language-supervision subset, whose captions rewrite supervision around grayscale structure and infrared-style contrast instead of RGB appearance. We show that the synthesized infrared imagery is markedly closer to real thermal imagery than a grayscale conversion on the AVIID benchmark. We fine-tune five CLIP backbones and six VLM backbones, and calibrate them against zero-shot behavior: IR-aware adaptation lifts CLIP mean recall by up to 12.8 points and drives VLM captioning IR-cue coverage to 100% while reducing residual RGB-color leakage to near zero. By isolating the infrared modality from RGB-IR dual-modal learning, MonoIR-RS offers a controlled, reproducible testbed for aligning infrared remote-sensing evidence with language.

</details>


### [64] [From RGB Generation to Dense Field Readout: Pixel-Space Dense Prediction with Text-to-Image Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06553)
*Zanyi Wang,Xin Lin,Haodong Li,Dengyang Jiang,Yijiang Li,Pengtao Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ReChannel方法，利用预训练的DiT（FLUX-Klein）进行密集预测任务。核心思想是：预训练DiT已建立patch-to-token-to-patch的格子结构，每个token索引固定输出patch，可直接映射到任务原生量（如深度、法线等），无需像现有方法那样将标注编码到VAE潜空间再解码回图像。ReChannel保留VAE编码器但去掉目标侧解码器，使用任务LoRA适配冻结的DiT，通过共享token局部线性头（约33K参数）将每个token映射到其p×p×K_t像素空间patch。在六项密集预测任务上评估，在trimap-free抠图、KITTI深度和指代分割上达到新SOTA，在法线、显著性、姿态上具有竞争力。在匹配的4B设置下，比编辑+潜解码方法更准确且快2.48倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法将密集预测视为目标生成任务，需要将标注编码到RGB训练的VAE潜空间再解码回图像，这继承了生成输出接口中密集预测不需要的部分。密集预测要求的是像素正确、任务原生的场，而不是生成新的RGB内容。本文观察到预训练DiT已组织好图像平面上的格子结构，应利用这一特性设计更高效的接口。

Method: ReChannel方法：1) 保留VAE编码器用于DiT输入分布，但去掉目标侧解码器；2) 使用任务LoRA适配冻结的DiT；3) 通过共享token局部线性头（约33K参数，无空间混合）将每个token映射到其p×p×K_t像素空间patch。使用FLUX-Klein作为骨干。

Result: 在六项密集预测任务上评估：trimap-free抠图、KITTI深度和指代分割达到新SOTA；在法线估计、显著性检测和姿态估计上具有竞争力。在匹配的4B设置下，比编辑+潜解码方法更准确且快2.48倍。

Conclusion: 密集预测可以从生成式预训练中获益，而无需继承其输出接口。ReChannel的极简接口设计证明了这一点。

Abstract: Large-scale text-to-image models are attractive backbones for dense prediction because RGB generation pretraining learns rich semantic, structural, and geometric priors. Existing generative and editing approaches reuse these priors by casting dense prediction as target generation: annotations such as depth, normals, alpha mattes, masks, and heatmaps are encoded into an RGB-trained VAE latent space and decoded back as image-like targets. We argue this inherits more of the generative output interface than dense prediction requires: unlike RGB synthesis, dense prediction asks for pixel-correct, task-native fields on the same image plane, not new RGB content to be rendered. Our key observation is that a pretrained DiT already organizes RGB inputs through a patch-to-token-to-patch lattice on the image plane, so each token indexes a fixed output patch whose channels can carry task-native quantities instead of RGB appearance. We instantiate this as ReChannel: we keep the VAE encoder for the DiT's input distribution but drop the target-side decoder, adapt the frozen DiT with task LoRA, and map each token to its p x p x K_t pixel-space patch through a shared token-local linear head--about 33K parameters, no spatial mixing. Using FLUX-Klein, we evaluate on six dense prediction tasks and over a dozen benchmarks. This minimal interface sets new state-of-the-art on trimap-free matting, KITTI depth, and referring segmentation, and stays competitive on normals, saliency, and pose. In a matched 4B setting it is more accurate and 2.48x faster than an edit-plus-latent-decode counterpart--dense perception can benefit from generative pretraining without inheriting its output interface.

</details>


### [65] [ProxyPose: 6-DoF Pose Tracking via Video-to-Video Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06555)
*Ruihang Zhang,Felix Taubner,Pooja Ravi,Kiriakos N. Kutulakos,David B. Lindell*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ProxyPose提出将6-DoF位姿跟踪重新定义为视频到视频的翻译问题，仅需单目视频和首帧中的一个标记像素，通过微调的视频扩散模型生成已知几何与外观的代理视频，再使用现成求解器恢复完整6-DoF轨迹，无需3D模型、深度图或物体掩码等额外输入，在挑战性场景（无纹理、透明、反光、形变表面）上达到SOTA精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的6-DoF位姿跟踪方法需要3D模型、深度图、物体掩码或任务特定特征等额外输入，且在处理无纹理、透明、反光或可形变表面时存在困难。作者希望仅从单目视频出发，无需额外输入即可实现鲁棒的6-DoF位姿跟踪。

Method: 将6-DoF位姿跟踪转化为视频到视频的翻译问题：给定一个视频和首帧中的一个标记像素，通过微调的视频扩散模型将输入视频翻译为代理视频（即一个颜色多面体经历与标记像素处表面区域相同的局部刚体运动的合成视频）。由于代理的几何和外观已知，恢复其完整6-DoF轨迹简化为经典位姿估计问题，可使用现成求解器。

Result: 在无需竞争方法所需的额外输入的情况下，实现了SOTA的6-DoF位姿跟踪精度；仅使用合成数据微调视频模型；扩展至人脸跟踪、相机位姿估计以及现有方法无法处理的野外挑战场景。

Conclusion: ProxyPose利用大规模视频预训练将位姿跟踪中最困难的方面（挑战性材质、遮挡、形变）吸收到翻译步骤中，在像素级别操作，不假设物体身份、边界或全局刚性，有效扩展了6-DoF位姿跟踪的适用范围。

Abstract: Tracking the six-degree-of-freedom (6-DoF) pose of objects and surfaces from monocular video is a long-standing problem in computer vision. To tackle this problem, existing methods require inputs beyond the video itself-such as 3D models, depth maps, object masks, or task-specific learned features-and they struggle with textureless, transparent, reflective, or deformable surfaces. Here, we introduce ProxyPose, which recasts 6-DoF pose tracking as video-to-video translation. Given only a video and a single marked pixel in the first frame, a fine-tuned video diffusion model translates the input into a proxy video-a synthetic video depicting a colored polyhedron undergoing the same local rigid-body motion as the surface region at the marked pixel. Because the proxy's geometry and appearance are known by construction, recovering its full 6-DoF trajectory reduces to classical pose estimation with off-the-shelf solvers. This formulation leverages large-scale video pre-training to absorb the hardest aspects of pose tracking-handling challenging materials, occlusions, and deformations-into the translation step, while operating at the pixel level with no assumptions about object identity, boundaries, or global rigidity. ProxyPose achieves state-of-the-art 6-DoF pose tracking accuracy without the additional inputs required by competing methods and after fine-tuning the video model only on synthetic data. We further demonstrate that ProxyPose extends to face tracking, camera pose estimation, and challenging in-the-wild scenes that are beyond the reach of existing approaches. Project page: https://ruihangzhang97.github.io/proxypose/.

</details>

### [66] [How Personas Can Influence Agents to Play Split or Steal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05398)
*Carlos Leon,Alexandre Rodrigues,Pedro Gamito,Thomas D. Parsons*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究了角色提示（persona prompts）在迭代"分或偷"（Split or Steal）博弈中对大语言模型智能体行为的影响。实验使用四个开源模型、两种温度设置，与固定提示的虚拟人类（VH）交互，进行了160轮次15回合的博弈（葡萄牙语）。结果显示：互惠Split结果占主导（~74%），剥削行为低于11%。模型选择显著影响行为：phi4和Ministral 3:3b保持合作，Gemma3:12b和Gemma4:e4b策略更多变。亲社会和原则性角色最合作，分析性角色更易剥削。友谊相关对话关联Split决策，金钱和报复关联Steal决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 角色提示常用于引导大语言模型智能体，但其在塑造社会困境中战略行为的有效性仍不确定，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在迭代Split or Steal游戏中，让角色提示驱动的智能体（四个开源模型：Ministral 3:3b、phi4:14b、Gemma3:12b、Gemma4:e4b，温度0.3/0.7/0）与固定提示的虚拟人类（GPT 4.1 mini）交互。进行160轮次15回合博弈，使用大五人格特征和主题分析来评估行为。

Result: 互惠Split结果占约74%轮次，剥削低于11%。phi4和Ministral 3:3b跨温度保持合作，Gemma3:12b和Gemma4:e4b策略更多变。亲社会和原则性角色最合作，分析性角色更易剥削。友谊话题关联Split，金钱和报复话题关联Steal。

Conclusion: 角色提示与模型差异在重复信任游戏中存在显著交互效应，为后续涉及人类参与者的虚拟现实研究建立了基线。

Abstract: Personas are often employed to guide large language model agents, yet their effectiveness in shaping strategic behavior in social dilemma settings remains uncertain. To address this, we examined the impact of persona prompts in an iterated Split or Steal game where persona-driven agents interacted with a Virtual Human (VH) controlled by a fixed prompt. Agents were instantiated from four open models (Ministral 3:3b, phi4:14b, Gemma3:12b, and Gemma4:e4b) at two temperature settings (0.3 and 0.7) and deterministic decision with zero temperature, while the VH was powered by GPT 4.1 mini. Across 160 sessions of 15 rounds each conducted in European Portuguese, mutual Split outcomes dominated (roughly 74 percent of rounds), with exploitation occurring in fewer than 11 percent of rounds. Model choice significantly influenced behavior: phi4 and Ministral 3:3b remained consistently cooperative across temperatures, whereas Gemma3:12b and Gemma4:e4b exhibited more varied strategies and outcomes. Analyses based on Big Five personality traits indicated that Prosocial and Principled personas were most consistently cooperative, while Analytical personas were more likely to exploit the VH. Topic analysis revealed that friendship-related dialogue aligns with Split decisions, whereas money and vengeance-related content is more prevalent in Steal outcomes; sentiment labels were predominantly neutral or happy and provided limited additional explanatory value. These findings characterize the interaction between persona prompts and model differences in repeated trust games and serve as a baseline for planned virtual reality studies involving human participants interacting with an embodied VH.

</details>


### [67] [Benchmarking KV-Cache Optimizations across Task Quality and System Performance for Long-Context Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05399)
*Nikita Agrawal,Ruben Mayer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文对四种KV缓存压缩机制（KIVI、TurboQuant、SnapKV、CaM）在长上下文场景下进行了工作负载感知的基准测试，发现压缩比不能预测端到端性能，不同机制在不同工作负载和模型上表现各异，建议根据具体工作负载选择压缩方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文工作负载下KV缓存增长成为LLM服务的主要瓶颈，但现有KV缓存压缩技术因在模型、任务、预算和服务栈上的评估不一致而难以比较，缺乏统一的基准。

Method: 在LongBench风格的多文档QA、单文档QA、少样本学习和摘要任务上，使用Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct和Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3模型，对量化（KIVI、TurboQuant）、剪枝（SnapKV）、合并（CaM）四种机制进行工作负载感知的基准测试，测量任务质量、平均输出吞吐量、平均首token延迟和实际压缩比。

Result: 压缩比不能有效预测端到端性能。KIVI4在跨模型质量上最稳定，SnapKV在长上下文吞吐量上表现最好，CaM在特定QA工作负载上收益大但对工作负载敏感。

Conclusion: KV缓存压缩应基于工作负载感知选择而非一刀切方案，为长上下文服务系统提供部署指导。

Abstract: Large language model serving is increasingly limited by KV-cache growth under long-context workloads, yet existing KV-cache compression techniques are difficult to compare because they were evaluated on different models, tasks, budgets, and serving stacks. This paper presents a workload-aware benchmark of representative KV-cache optimization mechanisms spanning quantization, pruning, and merging, including KIVI, TurboQuant, SnapKV, and CaM, evaluated on LongBench-style multi-document QA, single-document QA, few-shot learning, and summarization workloads using Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct and Mistral-7B-Instruct-v0.3. The benchmark measures task quality, mean output throughput, mean time-to-first-token, and realized compression ratio across context-length buckets. The results show that the compression ratio alone is a poor predictor of end-to-end performance. KIVI4 provides the most stable quality across models, SnapKV delivers the strongest long-context throughput, and CaM yields large gains on selected QA workloads but exhibits substantial workload sensitivity in both quality and realized compression ratio. These findings motivate workload-aware selection of KV-cache mechanisms rather than one-size-fits-all compression and provide deployment guidance for long-context serving systems.

</details>


### [68] [Text Distance from Nested and Hierarchical Repetitions: A Compression-Based Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05416)
*Xiaojun Hu,Jing Wang,Jingwen Zhang,Fengyao Zhai,Xiao Xie,Hao Liao,Zengru Di,Yu Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于算法信息论（AIT）的Ladderpath方法，通过提取语言序列中重复子结构的嵌套层次关系，定义三种距离度量（NCD及两种基于Ladderpath表示的距离），结合k近邻分类器，在分布内、分布外（OOD）和少样本文本分类任务中取得优异表现，尤其在OOD和低资源场景下超越gzip-NCD和BERT。该方法轻量、可解释、无需训练，展现了AIT方法在结构序列理解中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本分类方法（如基于深度学习的BERT）在分布外和低资源场景下表现受限，而传统的基于压缩的距离方法（如gzip-NCD）缺乏对序列结构的显式建模。作者希望从算法信息论出发，通过提取序列中重复子结构的层次关系，设计一种轻量、可解释且无需训练的结构化序列分析方法，以提升在资源受限和分布偏移场景下的文本分类性能。

Method: 基于算法信息论中的最小生成程序原则，提出Ladderpath方法，提取语言序列中的重复子结构并构建嵌套层次关系；在此基础上定义三种距离度量：归一化压缩距离（NCD）和两种直接源于Ladderpath表示的距离；集成k近邻（k-NN）分类器进行分类。

Result: 三种距离方法与k-NN结合，在分布内、分布外（OOD）和少样本文本分类任务上表现强劲且一致；在OOD和低资源设置下，所有三种方法均优于gzip-based NCD和BERT。

Conclusion: Ladderpath捕获的结构化表示能够保留序列的内在属性，提供了一种轻量、可解释且无需训练的文本建模替代方案，突显了基于算法信息论的方法在结构化和领域无关的序列理解中的潜力。

Abstract: We present a new method for structural sequence analysis grounded in Algorithmic Information Theory (AIT). At its core is the Ladderpath approach, which extracts nested and hierarchical relationships among repeated substructures in linguistic sequences -- an instantiation of AIT's principle of describing data through minimal generative programs. These structures are then used to define three distance measures: a normalized compression distance (NCD), and two alternative distances derived directly from the Ladderpath representation. Integrated with a $k$-nearest neighbor classifier, these distances achieve strong and consistent performance across in-distribution, out-of-distribution (OOD), and few-shot text classification tasks. In particular, all three methods outperform both gzip-based NCD and BERT under OOD and low-resource settings. These results demonstrate that the structured representations captured by Ladderpath preserve intrinsic properties of sequences and provide a lightweight, interpretable, and training-free alternative for text modeling. This work highlights the potential of AIT-based approaches for structural and domain-agnostic sequence understanding.

</details>


### [69] [Most LLM Conformity Needs No Speaker: Measuring the Speaker-Free Floor in Peer-Pressure Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05545)
*Yibo Hu,Jiaming Qu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM的所谓"从众行为"中，大部分在移除信息源（发言人）后仍然存在。现有基准混淆了"发言人存在"和"错误答案重复"两个线索。引入无源条件后，66.5%的正确案例发生有害修改，远高于重新提问的10.3%。来源框架主要调节这一基线水平。方法学启示：从众基准应先测量移除发言人后仍存在的效应，否则可能将重复文本误判为社会影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM从众基准将"发言人的存在"与"错误答案的重复"两个线索混合在一起，无法区分模型答案更改在多大程度上真正依赖于社交因素（发言人），而非仅仅是重复文本本身的机械影响。

Method: 引入无源条件（no-source condition）：仅提供被断言的错误答案，但移除明确的发言人标识。在6个开源LLM和7个QA/推理数据集上，对比无源条件、标准从众提示（有发言人）、重新提问（plain re-ask）等条件下的答案修改率。同时进行释义（paraphrasing）、开放式设定（隐藏选项）、专家框架（expert-panel framing）、最小人称标签等消融实验。

Result: 无源条件下，66.5%的正确案例发生有害修改（vs. 重新提问10.3%）；释义后效果仍在；开放式设定下效果持续；专家框架提高基线，最小人称标签则不能可靠提高；模型翻转时通常置信度很高，简单重新校准无法恢复原答案。

Conclusion: LLM的大部分"从众"行为实际上源于重复错误答案本身的文本效应，而非社会影响。来源归属仍有效应，但应作为超越无源基线的增量来测量。方法学教训：从众基准必须先测量移除发言人后的基线效应，否则可能将重复文本误判为社会影响。

Abstract: LLM conformity is often used to describe cases where a model changes a correct answer toward a peer or group response. We show that most of this apparent conformity survives even after the peer is removed. The reason is a confound: standard conformity prompts mix two cues at once, the presence of a speaker and the repeated wrong answer itself. Existing benchmarks vary these cues together, so they cannot tell how much of the revision actually depends on the speaker. We introduce a no-source condition: the same asserted answer with the explicit speaker removed. Across six open-weight LLMs and seven QA and reasoning datasets, this condition alone causes harmful revision in $66.5\%$ of initially correct cases, compared with $10.3\%$ under a plain re-ask. The effect also remains when the repeated answer is paraphrased and when answer options are hidden in an open-ended setting. Source framing mainly modulates this floor: expert-panel framing raises it, while minimal person labels do not reliably raise it. When models flip, they are usually confidently wrong, and simple recalibration does not recover the original answer. Source attribution still matters, but it should be measured as an increment above this speaker-free floor. The methodological lesson is that conformity benchmarks should first measure what remains after the speaker is removed; without this step, benchmarks may mistake repeated text for social influence.

</details>


### [70] [The yes-no bias of large language models reflects answer order and wording, not shifts in moral judgment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.05552)
*Haonan Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LLM在道德困境中存在顺序偏差（偏向最后选项）和"不"字的词汇吸引效应，而非人类的首因效应或逻辑否决偏差；通过交叉对称化心理测量方法可分离出模型真实的道德立场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究发现LLM在道德困境上的判断会因措辞的逻辑无关变化而改变（如yes-no偏差放大），但单一框架无法区分这种偏移究竟是逻辑裁决、词汇标记还是选项顺序所致。需要一种方法分离这些因素。

Method: 引入交叉对称化（crossed symmetrization）心理测量方法：将所有逻辑无关因素（选项顺序、措辞标签等）以平衡配对方式交叉，使用分级评分而非二元判决，跨语料库问题形式恢复模型内部道德尺度。提出最小模型P = σ((θ±m)/s)量化框架敏感性m和道德决断性s。

Result: 前沿模型的道德立场近乎格式不变（跨形式不连贯性0.12-0.21）；通过yes/no强迫判决时存在可分解的人为偏差：偏向最后选项的顺序偏差（与人类首因效应相反）和词汇"不"的吸引效应。Claude模型人为偏差显著（-0.32至-0.86），GPT-5.5和Gemini≈0；扩展推理后减小。逻辑否决偏差对前沿模型≈0，偏差跟随印刷表面而非裁决本身。

Conclusion: 测量模型的价值取向需要交叉变换问题框架，而非单一询问。所提方法适用于任何困境集和二元格式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly issue judgments read as binary verdicts, and a growing literature reports such judgments shifting under logically irrelevant changes of wording - among them an amplified yes-no bias on moral dilemmas, absent in humans. A single framing cannot say what such a shift is: in a yes/no question the word "no" is at once logical verdict, lexical token, and last-printed option. We introduce a psychometric battery that separates these: crossed symmetrization - every logically irrelevant factor flipped in balanced pairs - across a corpus of question forms. A graded rating across logically equivalent forms recovers a coherent internal moral scale: frontier models' stance $θ$ is nearly format-invariant (cross-form incoherence 0.12-0.21 on a $\pm 1$ axis); small open-weight models fail in model-specific ways.
